### 2b — Taxable interest

Second:

{"collection": "interest", "field": "interest.amount", "op": "sum_over", "where": null}

Second transcription's note: The instructions say 'Enter your total taxable interest income on line 2b'. I read this as the total over the payer entries. The OID, market discount and bond premium adjustments go through the Schedule B screen.

Ruling: equivalent representation; project retained. The project enters Schedule B line 4 (total interest less excludable savings-bond interest, which the project refuses and so is zero); the second transcription totals the interest payers directly. Schedule B line 2 is the same total over the same payers (Schedule B (2025), lines 1–4), so the two agree within scope.

Ruling: equivalent representation; project retained. Schedule B line 6 is the total of line 5, which is the Form 1099-DIV box 1a total the second transcription copied directly (`sb.ordinary_dividends`); the project reaches the same value through Schedule B (Schedule B (2025), lines 5–6).

Ruling: representation; project retained. The election is for a social security benefit payment for an earlier year, so it can only apply to a filer with benefits; the project tests benefits first (the 'all' stops there) so a filer without benefits is never asked the lump-sum question, and for a filer with benefits the box is the answer itself.

Ruling: scope and representation; project retained. As the earlier ruling: Form 8814, Form 4972 and the box 3 tax are refused through scope.other_line16. Ruling: scope; project retained. The second adds the filer's choice not to claim the EIC as an input; the tool does not ask it (the line 27c label says so) and enters the credit whenever Steps 1 to 5 pass.

### 27b — Clergy filing Schedule SE (see instructions)
Status: **AGREE**

### 27c — If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here
Status: **DISAGREE**

Second transcription's note: 'Check the box on line 27c if you do not want to claim the earned income credit or if you have been instructed to check the box in the instructions for line 27a.'

Ruling: scope; project retained. As line 27a: the choice not to claim is not asked; the instructed checks agree in substance (see eic.check_27c).

### Dependents (separated spouses) — Check if your filing status is MFS or HOH and you lived apart from your spouse for the las
Status: **AGREE**

### 28 — Additional child tax credit (ACTC) from Schedule 8812
Status: **AGREE**

### 29 — American opportunity credit from Form 8863, line 8
Status: **AGREE**

### 30 — Refundable adoption credit from Form 8839, line 13
Status: **AGREE**

### 31 — Amount from Schedule 3, line 15
Status: **AGREE**

### 32 — Add lines 27a, 28, 29, 30, and 31. These are your total other payments and refundable cred
Status: **AGREE**

### 33 — Add lines 25d, 26, and 32. These are your total payments
Status: **AGREE**

### 34 — If line 33 is more than line 24, subtract line 24 from line 33. This is the amount you ove
Status: **AGREE**

### 37 — Subtract line 33 from line 24. This is the amount you owe
Status: **AGREE**

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: f1040.line1a
    printed: "1a"
    label: "Total amount from Form(s) W-2, box 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.box1}
    note: "The instructions say: 'Enter the total amount from Form(s) W-2, box 1. If a joint return, also include your spouse's income from Form(s) W-2, box 1.' I summed the per-person box 1 totals, which are themselves totals over each person's W-2 entries."

  - id: f1040.line1z
    printed: "1z"
    label: "Add lines 1a through 1h"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1a}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "Lines 1b through 1h cover household employee wages, unreported tips, Medicaid waiver payments, taxable dependent care benefits, adoption benefits, Form 8919 wages and other earned income. The tool does not compute any of these, so I encoded only line 1a and refuse through the other-income screen. I did not write those lines as zero."

  - id: f1040.line2b
    printed: "2b"
    label: "Taxable interest"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: interest, field: interest.amount}
    refuses: [sb.unsupported]
    note: "The instructions say 'Enter your total taxable interest income on line 2b'. I read this as the total over the payer entries. The OID, market discount and bond premium adjustments go through the Schedule B screen."

  - id: f1040.line3b
    printed: "3b"
    label: "Ordinary dividends"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sb.ordinary_dividends}
    refuses: [sb.unsupported]
    note: "The instructions say 'Enter your total ordinary dividends on line 3b. This amount should be shown in box 1a of Form(s) 1099-DIV.' Nominee dividends are screened out."

  - id: f1040.line4a
    printed: "4a"
    label: "IRA distributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.amount_a
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [retirement.special]
    note: "The total distribution goes on line 4a only when an exception applies. A fully taxable distribution goes only on line 4b ('don't make an entry on line 4a'). retirement.amount_a carries that per-entry rule."

  - id: f1040.line4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "IRA distributions, taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.taxable
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [retirement.special]

  - id: f1040.h_ira_rollover_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of IRA distributions that were partly or wholly rolled over (Exception 1)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.has_rollover, {const: true}]}

  - id: f1040.line4c
    printed: "4c"
    label: "Check if (box 1) Rollover"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f1040.h_ira_rollover_count, {const: 0}]}
    note: "'If Exception 1 applies to you, check box 1 on line 4c.' Boxes 2 (QCD) and 3 (HFD and others) are outside this line's scope."

  - id: f1040.line5a
    printed: "5a"
    label: "Pensions and annuities"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.amount_a
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}
    refuses: [retirement.special]

  - id: f1040.line5b
    printed: "5b"
    label: "Pensions and annuities, taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.taxable
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}
    refuses: [retirement.special]

  - id: f1040.h_plan_rollover_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of pension or annuity distributions with a rollover, including a direct rollover"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.has_rollover, {const: true}]}

  - id: f1040.line5c
    printed: "5c"
    label: "Check if (box 1) Rollover"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f1040.h_plan_rollover_count, {const: 0}]}
    note: "'If you have a rollover, including a direct rollover, from one qualified employer's plan to another or to an IRA, check box 1 on line 5c.'"

  - id: f1040.line6a
    printed: "6a"
    label: "Social security benefits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: ssb.line1}

  - id: f1040.line6b
    printed: "6b"
    label: "Social security benefits, taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: ssb.line18}
    refuses: [ssb.pub915, ssb.lump_sum]
    note: "The worksheet text routes the figure through the STOP exits at worksheet lines 7 and 9 ('Enter -0- on line 6b'). I assumed ssb.line18 carries those exits. The worksheet exceptions and the lump-sum election are refused."

  - id: f1040.line6c
    printed: "6c"
    label: "If you elect to use the lump-sum election method, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: ssb.lump_sum}

  - id: f1040.line6d
    printed: "6d"
    label: "If you are married filing separately and lived apart from your spouse the entire year, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [ssb.lived_apart, {const: true}]}

  - id: f1040.line7a
    printed: "7a"
    label: "Capital gain or (loss). Attach Schedule D if required"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sd.to1040}

  - id: f1040.line8
    printed: "8"
    label: "Additional income from Schedule 1, line 10"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "Schedule 1 is not computed. The zero holds only after the Schedule 1 screen is answered no."

  - id: f1040.line9
    printed: "9"
    label: "Add lines 1z, 2b, 3b, 4b, 5b, 6b, 7a, and 8. This is your total income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line1z, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, f1040.line4b, f1040.line5b, f1040.line6b, f1040.line7a, f1040.line8]}

  - id: f1040.line10
    printed: "10"
    label: "Adjustments to income from Schedule 1, line 26"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]

  - id: f1040.line11a
    printed: "11a"
    label: "Subtract line 10 from line 9. This is your adjusted gross income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1040.line9, take: f1040.line10}

  - id: f1040.line11b
    printed: "11b"
    label: "Amount from line 11a (adjusted gross income)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11a}

  - id: f1040.line12e
    printed: "12e"
    label: "Standard deduction or itemized deductions (from Schedule A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sa.deduction}

  - id: f1040.line13a
    printed: "13a"
    label: "Qualified business income deduction from Form 8995 or Form 8995-A"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: qbi.deduction}
    refuses: [qbi.unsupported]

  - id: f1040.line13b
    printed: "13b"
    label: "Additional deductions from Schedule 1-A, line 38"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1a]

  - id: f1040.line14
    printed: "14"
    label: "Add lines 12e, 13a, and 13b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line12e, f1040.line13a, f1040.line13b]}

  - id: f1040.line15
    printed: "15"
    label: "Subtract line 14 from line 11b. If zero or less, enter -0-. This is your taxable income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line11b, take: f1040.line14}

  - id: f1040.h_regular_tax
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Tax on line 15 from the Tax Table or Tax Computation Worksheet"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: tax_on_amount, bind: {amount: f1040.line15, status: f1040.status}, line: tax}}

  - id: f1040.h_no_worksheet
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Exception: use neither the Qualified Dividends and Capital Gain Tax Worksheet nor the Schedule D Tax Worksheet (Schedule D line 15 or 16 zero or less and no qualified dividends, or Form 1040 line 15 zero or less)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, le: [f1040.line15, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, le: [qdcg.line3, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.line3a, {const: 0}]}
    note: "The exception names Schedule D lines 15 and 16. I used qdcg.line3 for the gain: the smaller of those lines, floored at zero, when Schedule D is filed, and otherwise line 7a. The exception text does not say what happens when no Schedule D is filed. With no gain and no qualified dividends the worksheet tax equals the regular tax anyway."

  - id: f1040.h_use_sdtw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Complete the Schedule D Tax Worksheet: Schedule D line 18 or 19 more than zero and lines 15 and 16 gains, or filing Form 4952 with an amount on line 4g"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [sd.line17, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, gt: [sd.line18, {const: 0}]}
                - {op: test, gt: [sd.line19, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [sd.filing4952, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [f4952.line4g, {const: 0}]}

  - id: f1040.h_guard_sdtw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Tax from the Schedule D Tax Worksheet"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_no_worksheet, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_use_sdtw, {const: true}]}

  - id: f1040.h_guard_qdcg
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Tax from the Qualified Dividends and Capital Gain Tax Worksheet"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_no_worksheet, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_use_sdtw, {const: false}]}

  - id: f1040.line16
    printed: "16"
    label: "Tax (see instructions). Check if any from Form(s): 1 8814 2 4972 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_no_worksheet, {const: true}]}, then: f1040.h_regular_tax}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_guard_sdtw, {const: true}]}, then: sdtw.line47}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_guard_qdcg, {const: true}]}, then: qdcg.line25}
    refuses: [scope.other_line16]
    note: "The line 16 instructions describing the tax methods are cut off in the supplied text. I chose the method from the Schedule D Tax Worksheet's own conditions and exception. Taxes from Forms 8814 and 4972, the section 962 election, education credit recapture, Form 8621 and Form 8978 are refused, not added as zero."

  - id: f1040.line17
    printed: "17"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 2, line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s2.line3}

  - id: f1040.line18
    printed: "18"
    label: "Add lines 16 and 17"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line16, f1040.line17]}

  - id: f1040.line19
    printed: "19"
    label: "Child tax credit or credit for other dependents from Schedule 8812"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s8812.line14}
    note: "The form does not name the Schedule 8812 line. I used line 14 ('This is your child tax credit')."

  - id: f1040.line20
    printed: "20"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 3, line 8"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s3.line8}

  - id: f1040.line21
    printed: "21"
    label: "Add lines 19 and 20"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line19, f1040.line20]}

  - id: f1040.line22
    printed: "22"
    label: "Subtract line 21 from line 18. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line18, take: f1040.line21}

  - id: f1040.line23
    printed: "23"
    label: "Other taxes, including self-employment tax, from Schedule 2, line 21"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s2.line21}

  - id: f1040.line24
    printed: "24"
    label: "Add lines 22 and 23. This is your total tax"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line22, f1040.line23]}

  - id: f1040.line25a
    printed: "25a"
    label: "Federal income tax withheld from Form(s) W-2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.box2}

  - id: f1040.line25d
    printed: "25d"
    label: "Add lines 25a through 25c"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line25a, f1040.line25b, f1040.line25c]}

  - id: f1040.h_decline_eic
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Do you not want to claim the earned income credit?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: input, source: "Form 1040 line 27c: If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here"}
    note: "The form gives an election to decline the EIC, and no existing input carries it, so I added this interview input."

  - id: f1040.line27a
    printed: "27a"
    label: "Earned income credit (EIC)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_decline_eic, {const: true}]}, then: eic.zero}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_decline_eic, {const: false}]}, then: eic.credit}

  - id: f1040.line27b
    printed: "27b"
    label: "Clergy filing Schedule SE (see instructions)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.clergy}

  - id: f1040.line27c
    printed: "27c"
    label: "If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_decline_eic, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.check_27c, {const: true}]}
    note: "'Check the box on line 27c if you do not want to claim the earned income credit or if you have been instructed to check the box in the instructions for line 27a.'"

  - id: "f1040.lineDependents (separated spouses)"
    printed: "Dependents (separated spouses)"
    label: "Check if your filing status is MFS or HOH and you lived apart from your spouse for the last 6 months of 2025, or you are legally separated according to your state law under a written separation agreement or a decree of separate maintenance and you did not live in the same household as your spouse at the end of 2025."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.separated_box}
    note: "This id follows the required f1040.line<printed> pattern literally, so it contains spaces. Under the EIC Step 3 instructions, the box is checked when you answer No to question 6 after Step 3's separated-spouse questions."

  - id: f1040.line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Additional child tax credit (ACTC) from Schedule 8812"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s8812.line27}

  - id: f1040.line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "American opportunity credit from Form 8863, line 8"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.credits]

  - id: f1040.line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "Refundable adoption credit from Form 8839, line 13"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.credits]

  - id: f1040.line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 3, line 15"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s3.line15}

  - id: f1040.line32
    printed: "32"
    label: "Add lines 27a, 28, 29, 30, and 31. These are your total other payments and refundable credits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line27a, f1040.line28, f1040.line29, f1040.line30, f1040.line31]}

  - id: f1040.line33
    printed: "33"
    label: "Add lines 25d, 26, and 32. These are your total payments"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line25d, f1040.line26, f1040.line32]}

  - id: f1040.line34
    printed: "34"
    label: "If line 33 is more than line 24, subtract line 24 from line 33. This is the amount you overpaid"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line33, take: f1040.line24}
    note: "When line 33 is not more than line 24, the line is left blank. I encoded that as zero, which is what subtract_floor0 gives."

  - id: f1040.line37
    printed: "37"
    label: "Subtract line 33 from line 24. This is the amount you owe"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line24, take: f1040.line33}
    note: "The printed line has no zero floor. But when line 33 is more than line 24 there is no amount owed, because the overpayment goes on line 34, so I floored this line at zero. Line 38 (estimated tax penalty) is not in scope."
```
```
